```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

_TIMEOUT_DEADLINE = None


class _OptTimeout(Exception):
    pass


def _timeout_callback(xk):
    if _TIMEOUT_DEADLINE is not None and time.time() > _TIMEOUT_DEADLINE:
        raise _OptTimeout()


def compute_all_lags(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * (2.0 / float(n))


def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(compute_all_lags(h, n)))


def _exact_sum_adjust(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    total = float(h.sum())
    delta = target - total
    if abs(delta) < 1e-15:
        return h

    candidates = h + delta
    ok = (candidates >= 0.0) & (candidates <= 1.0)
    if np.any(ok):
        idxs = np.where(ok)[0]
        dist = np.abs(h - 0.5)
        pos = int(np.argmin(dist[idxs]))
        h[idxs[pos]] = candidates[idxs[pos]]
        return h

    for _ in range(5):
        total = float(h.sum())
        delta = target - total
        if abs(delta) < 1e-15:
            break
        if delta > 0.0:
            free = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)[0]
        else:
            free = np.where(h > 1e-12)[0]
        if len(free) == 0:
            break
        h[free] = np.clip(h[free] + delta / float(len(free)), 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def project_box_sum(y, n):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    x = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(x.sum()) - target) < 1e-14:
        return _exact_sum_adjust(x, n)

    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        x = np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(x.sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    x = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return _exact_sum_adjust(x, n)


def project_fast(y, n):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(y, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    diff = target - float(x.sum())
    for _ in range(12):
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            free = x < (1.0 - 1e-12)
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            x[free] = np.minimum(1.0, x[free] + diff / float(cnt))
        else:
            free = x > 1e-12
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            x[free] = np.maximum(0.0, x[free] + diff / float(cnt))
        diff = target - float(x.sum())
    return x


def smooth_value_weights(vals, alpha):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    shifted = alpha * (vals - m)
    mx = float(np.max(shifted))
    ex = np.exp(shifted - mx)
    z = float(np.sum(ex))
    if z <= 0.0:
        return m, None
    weights = ex / z
    obj = m + (mx + float(np.log(z))) / alpha
    return float(obj), weights


def gradient_full_weights(h, n, weights):
    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    w0 = float(weights[center]) if weights.size > center else 0.0

    wp = np.zeros(n)
    wn = np.zeros(n)
    if weights.size > n:
        wp[1:] = weights[n:min(weights.size, 2 * n - 1)]
    if n - 1 > 0 and weights.size >= n - 1:
        wn[1:] = weights[:n - 1][::-1]

    g = w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    upper_const = np.cumsum(wp)
    lower_const = np.cumsum(wn)[::-1]
    s = wp + wn
    if np.any(s):
        left = np.convolve(h, s, mode='full')[:n]
        right = np.convolve(h, s[::-1], mode='full')[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        g = g + upper_const + lower_const - (left + right)
    else:
        g = g + upper_const + lower_const
    return dx * g


def gradient_sparse(h, n, active, weights):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    g = np.zeros(n)
    center = n - 1
    for idx, w in zip(active, weights):
        w = float(w)
        if w <= 1e-15:
            continue
        idx = int(idx)
        lag = idx - center
        if lag == 0:
            g += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        else:
            m = abs(lag)
            if m >= n:
                continue
            if lag > 0:
                g[:n - m] -= w * h[m:]
                g[m:] += w * (1.0 - h[:n - m])
            else:
                g[:n - m] += w * (1.0 - h[m:])
                g[m:] -= h[:n - m]
    return dx * g


def smooth_obj_value(x, n, active, alpha, rho=0.0):
    c = compute_all_lags(x, n)
    vals = c if active is None else c[active]
    obj, _ = smooth_value_weights(vals, alpha)
    if rho != 0.0:
        diff = x.sum() - 0.5 * float(n)
        obj += rho * float(diff) * float(diff)
    return float(obj)


def smooth_grad(x, n, active, alpha, rho=0.0):
    c = compute_all_lags(x, n)
    if active is None:
        vals = c
        _, weights = smooth_value_weights(vals, alpha)
        g = gradient_full_weights(x, n, weights)
    else:
        vals = c[active]
        _, weights = smooth_value_weights(vals, alpha)
        if len(active) > 25:
            wf = np.zeros(len(c))
            wf[active] = weights
            g = gradient_full_weights(x, n, wf)
        else:
            g = gradient_sparse(x, n, active, weights)
    if rho != 0.0:
        diff = x.sum() - 0.5 * float(n)
        g += 2.0 * rho * diff
    return g


def top_indices(values, k):
    values = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
    k = min(int(k), len(values))
    if k <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if k >= len(values):
        return np.arange(len(values), dtype=int)
    return np.argpartition(values, len(values) - k)[-k:].astype(int)


def active_from_lags(c, k, tol=0.0, include_center=True):
    length = len(c)
    center = length // 2
    if k <= 0 or length == 0:
        return np.array([center], dtype=int)
    idx = top_indices(c, k)
    if tol > 0.0:
        idx2 = np.where(c >= c.max() - tol)[0]
        idx = np.concatenate([idx, idx2])
    idx = np.unique(idx)
    opp = 2 * (length - 1) - idx
    if include_center:
        idx = np.concatenate([idx, opp, [center]])
    return np.unique(idx).astype(int)


def single_lag_grad(h, n, lag):
    g = np.zeros(n)
    if lag == 0:
        return 1.0 - 2.0 * h
    m = abs(lag)
    if m >= n:
        return g
    if lag > 0:
        g[:n - m] -= h[m:]
        g[m:] += 1.0 - h[:n - m]
    else:
        g[:n - m] += 1.0 - h[m:]
        g[m:] -= h[:n - m]
    return g


def _sum_con(x, n):
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * float(n))


def _sum_con_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones_like(x)


def lbfbsb_safe(h, n, alpha, active, rho, maxiter, deadline, maxcall_s=80.0):
    global _TIMEOUT_DEADLINE
    if maxiter <= 0:
        return h, compute_c5(h, n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    c0 = compute_c5(h, n)

    bounds = []
    for _ in range(n):
        bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))

    tlimit = min(deadline - 1.0, time.time() + float(maxcall_s))
    use_callback = tlimit > time.time() + 0.5
    if use_callback:
        _TIMEOUT_DEADLINE = tlimit

    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_obj_value,
            h,
            args=(n, active, alpha, rho),
            method='L-BFGS-B',
            bounds=bounds,
            jac=smooth_grad,
            callback=_timeout_callback if use_callback else None,
            options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-12, 'maxls': 60, 'disp': False}
        )
    except _OptTimeout:
        h = project_box_sum(h, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h, n)
    except Exception:
        h = project_box_sum(h, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h, n)
    finally:
        _TIMEOUT_DEADLINE = None

    if res is None or not hasattr(res, 'x') or res.x is None:
        return h, c0
    h_new = project_box_sum(res.x, n)
    c_new = compute_c5(h_new, n)
    if c_new < c0 - 1e-15:
        return h_new, c_new
    return h, c0


def slsqp_active(h, n, active, alpha, maxiter=200):
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    c0 = compute_c5(h, n)
    bounds = []
    for _ in range(n):
        bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _sum_con, 'jac': _sum_con_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_obj_value,
            h,
            args=(n, active, alpha, 0.0),
            method='SLSQP',
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            jac=smooth_grad,
            options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-12, 'disp': False}
        )
    except Exception:
        return h, c0
    if res is None or not hasattr(res, 'x') or res.x is None:
        return h, c0
    h_new = project_box_sum(res.x, n)
    c_new = compute_c5(h_new, n)
    if c_new < c0 - 1e-15:
        return h_new, c_new
    return h, c0


def add_point_delta(out, h, n, p, delta):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    out[p:p + n] += delta * (1.0 - h[::-1])
    out[n - 1 - p:2 * n - 1 - p] += -delta * h


def candidate_pair_c(h, n, c, i, j, di, dj):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    out = c.copy()
    if di != 0.0:
        add_point_delta(out, h, n, i, di * dx)
    if dj != 0.0:
        add_point_delta(out, h, n, j, dj * dx)

    center = n - 1
    quad = dx * (di * di + dj * dj)
    if quad != 0.0:
        out[center] -= quad

    cross = dx * di * dj
    if cross != 0.0:
        out[center + (j - i)] -= cross
        out[center + (i - j)] -= cross
    return out


def pair_local_search(h, n, deadline):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    c_arr = compute_all_lags(h, n)
    best_c = float(np.max(c_arr))
    current_c = best_c
    center = n - 1

    deltas = (
        0.15,
        0.075,
        0.0375,
        0.01875,
        0.009375,
        0.0046875,
        0.00234375,
        0.001171875,
        5e-4,
        2e-4
    )
    top_lags = max(3, min(10, len(c_arr) // 50))
    pair_count = max(20, min(80, n // 8))
    fracs = (1.0, 0.618, 0.382, 0.15)

    for d in deltas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 0.5:
            break
        improved = True
        while improved and time.time() < deadline - 0.5:
            improved = False
            c_arr = compute_all_lags(h, n)
            current_c = float(np.max(c_arr))
            if current_c < best_c:
                best_c = current_c

            top_idx = top_indices(c_arr, top_lags)
            found = False
            for idx in top_idx:
                if found:
                    break
                idx = int(idx)
                lag = idx - center
                if lag == 0:
                    continue
                m = abs(lag)
                if m >= n:
                    continue

                if lag > 0:
                    diffs = h[m:] - h[:n - m]
                else:
                    diffs = h[:n - m] - h[m:]

                pc = pair_count
                if pc > n - m:
                    pc = max(1, n - m)
                order = np.argsort(diffs)[-pc:]

                for pos in order[::-1]:
                    if time.time() > deadline - 0.5:
                        break
                    i = int(pos)
                    j = i + m
                    if lag > 0:
                        diff = h[j] - h[i]
                        if diff <= 1e-12:
                            continue
                        base = min(float(d), 0.5 * float(diff), float(h[j]), 1.0 - float(h[i]))
                        sign = 1.0
                    else:
                        diff = h[i] - h[j]
                        if diff <= 1e-12:
                            continue
                        base = min(float(d), 0.5 * float(diff), float(h[i]), 1.0 - float(h[j]))
                        sign = -1.0

                    if base <= 1e-11:
                        continue

                    old_i = float(h[i])
                    old_j = float(h[j])
                    old_current = current_c
                    best_s = 0.0
                    best_val = current_c + 1.0

                    for frac in fracs:
                        s = base * float(frac)
                        if s <= 1e-12:
                            continue
                        out = candidate_pair_c(h, n, c_arr, i, j, sign * s, -sign * s)
                        val = float(np.max(out))
                        if val < best_val:
                            best_val = val
                            best_s = s

                    if best_s > 0.0 and best_val < old_current - 1e-13:
                        h[i] = old_i + sign * best_s
                        h[j] = old_j - sign * best_s
                        c_check = compute_all_lags(h, n)
                        val_check = float(np.max(c_check))
                        if val_check < old_current - 1e-13:
                            c_arr = c_check
                            current_c = val_check
                            if current_c < best_c:
                                best_c = current_c
                            improved = True
                            found = True
                            break
                        else:
                            h[i] = old_i
                            h[j] = old_j

            if found:
                h = project_fast(h, n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    return h, best_c


def projected_gradient_polish(h, n, deadline, K=20, alphas=(100.0, 1000.0, 5000.0)):
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        h_curr = best_h.copy()
        step = float(np.clip(1.0 / np.sqrt(float(alpha)), 0.01, 0.25))
        active = None
        active_timer = 0
        no_accept = 0

        for _ in range(2000):
            if time.time() > deadline - 1.0:
                break
            c = compute_all_lags(h_curr, n)
            cur_c5 = float(np.max(c))
            if cur_c5 > best_c + 1e-5:
                h_curr = best_h.copy()
                step = max(step, 0.02)
                continue

            if active is None or active_timer >= 20:
                active = active_from_lags(c, K)
                active_timer = 0
            else:
                active_timer += 1

            vals = c if active is None else c[active]
            F, weights = smooth_value_weights(vals, alpha)

            if active is None:
                g = gradient_full_weights(h_curr, n, weights)
            else:
                if len(active) > 25:
                    wf = np.zeros(len(c))
                    wf[active] = weights
                    g = gradient_full_weights(h_curr, n, wf)
                else:
                    g = gradient_sparse(h_curr, n, active, weights)

            gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if gnorm < 1e-13:
                break

            t = step
            accepted = False
            best_cand = None
            best_c2 = None

            for _ls in range(10):
                cand = project_fast(h_curr - t * g, n)
                disp = cand - h_curr
                if float(np.dot(disp, disp)) < 1e-16:
                    break
                c2 = compute_all_lags(cand, n)
                vals2 = c2 if active is None else c2[active]
                F2, _ = smooth_value_weights(vals2, alpha)
                dot = float(np.dot(g, disp))
                if F2 <= F + 1e-4 * t * dot + 1e-12:
                    accepted = True
                    best_cand = cand
                    best_c2 = c2
                    break
                t *= 0.5
                if t < 1e-8:
                    break

            if accepted:
                h_curr = best_cand
                c5 = float(np.max(best_c2))
                if c5 < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_c = c5
                    best_h = h_curr.copy()
                step = min(0.5, step * 1.1)
                no_accept = 0
            else:
                no_accept += 1
                step *= 0.5
                if no_accept > 40 or step < 1e-8:
                    break

    h = best_h
    c = compute_c5(h, n)
    return h, c


def linprog_cut_step(h, n, active, trust):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    c_arr = compute_all_lags(h, n)
    current = float(np.max(c_arr))
    if len(active) == 0:
        return h, current

    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    center = n - 1
    rows = []
    rhs = []
    for idx in active:
        idx = int(idx)
        if idx < 0 or idx >= len(c_arr):
            continue
        lag = idx - center
        grad = single_lag_grad(h, n, lag)
        col = dx * grad
        row = np.concatenate((col, np.array([-1.0])))
        rows.append(row)
        rhs.append(float(np.dot(col, h)) - c_arr[idx])

    if not rows:
        return h, current

    A_ub = np.array(rows)
    b_ub = np.array(rhs)
    A_eq = np.ones((1, n + 1))
    A_eq[0, -1] = 0.0
    b_eq = np.array([0.5 * float(n)])

    bounds = []
    for i in range(n):
        lo = float(h[i]) - trust
        up = float(h[i]) + trust
        if lo < 0.0:
            lo = 0.0
        if up > 1.0:
            up = 1.0
        if up < lo:
            up = lo
        bounds.append((lo, up))
    bounds.append((0.0, None))

    obj = np.zeros(n + 1)
    obj[-1] = 1.0
    idxs = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    obj[:-1] += 1e-7 * np.sin(0.0137 * idxs + 1.2345)

    try:
        res = linprog(obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds=bounds)
        except Exception:
            return h, current

    if res is not None and res.success and res.x is not None:
        x = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
        x = project_box_sum(x, n)
        c_new = compute_c5(x, n)
        return x, c_new
    return h, current


def cut_polish(h, n, deadline, max_rounds=4):
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    current = compute_c5(h, n)
    trust = 0.12
    for _r in range(max_rounds):
        if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        c_arr = compute_all_lags(h, n)
        active = active_from_lags(c_arr, 15)
        x_new, c_new = linprog_cut_step(h, n, active, trust)
        if c_new < current - 1e-13:
            h, current = x_new, c_new
            trust *= 0.8
        else:
            trust *= 0.7
            if trust < 0.02:
                break
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    current = compute_c5(h, n)
    return h, current


def optimize_at_n(h_start, n, deadline):
    h = project_box_sum(h_start, n)
    c = compute_c5(h, n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 50:
        if n <= 800:
            if n <= 400:
                full_runs = ((50.0, 260, 80.0), (200.0, 200, 60.0))
            else:
                full_runs = ((50.0, 120, 65.0), (200.0, 100, 45.0))
            for alpha, maxit, call_s in full_runs:
                if time.time() > deadline - 40:
                    break
                h_new, c_new = lbfbsb_safe(h, n, alpha, None, 1000.0, maxit, deadline - 10, call_s)
                if c_new < c - 1e-14:
                    h, c = h_new, c_new

    if time.time() < deadline - 40:
        for K in (6, 15, 40):
            if time.time() > deadline - 30:
                break
            c_arr = compute_all_lags(h, n)
            active = active_from_lags(c_arr, K)
            for alpha in (500.0, 3000.0):
                if time.time() > deadline - 25:
                    break
                maxit = 240 if n <= 400 else 100
                call_s = 45.0 if n <= 400 else 30.0
                h_new, c_new = lbfbsb_safe(h, n, alpha, active, 1000.0, maxit, deadline - 15, call_s)
                if c_new < c - 1e-14:
                    h, c = h_new, c_new

    if n <= 800 and time.time() < deadline - 40:
        sub = time.time() + min(80.0, max(10.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.35))
        sub = min(sub, deadline - 20.0)
        h, c = cut_polish(h, n, sub)

    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
        sub = time.time() + min(90.0, max(10.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.35))
        h, c = projected_gradient_polish(h, n, min(sub, deadline - 5), K=20, alphas=(200.0, 2000.0, 10000.0))

    if n <= 500 and time.time() < deadline - 15:
        c_arr = compute_all_lags(h, n)
        for K, alpha, maxit in ((5, 5000.0, 150), (10, 3000.0, 110)):
            if time.time() > deadline - 8:
                break
            active = active_from_lags(c_arr, K)
            h_new, c_new = slsqp_active(h, n, active, alpha, maxit)
            if c_new < c - 1e-14:
                h, c = h_new, c_new
            c_arr = compute_all_lags(h, n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        h, c = pair_local_search(h, n, deadline - 0.5)

    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    c = compute_c5(h, n)
    return h, c


def resample_nearest(h, n_old, n_new):
    if n_new == n_old:
        return h.copy()
    if n_old <= 1:
        return np.full(n_new, 0.5)
    idx = (np.arange(n_new) * n_old) // n_new
    idx = np.minimum(idx, n_old - 1)
    return h[idx]


def candidate_ns_for(base_n):
    base_n = int(base_n)
    prefs = []
    for mult in (0.65, 0.8, 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, 1.25, 1.5, 1.75, 2.0):
        prefs.append(int(round(base_n * mult)))
    prefs.extend([80, 120, 160, 200, 240, 280, 320, 360, 400, 450, 500, 550, 600, 700, 800, 1000])
    out = []
    for x in prefs:
        if 48 <= x <= 1000 and x not in out:
            out.append(x)
    return out


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0

    best_h = None
    best_c = float('inf')

    parent = globals().get('initial_h_values')
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            h0 = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float).copy()
        except Exception:
            h0 = None
        if h0 is not None and len(h0) > 0:
            n0 = len(h0)
            h0 = project_box_sum(h0, n0)
            c0 = compute_c5(h0, n0)
            best_h, best_c = h0, c0

    if best_h is None:
        n0 = 300
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0)
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.3 * np.sin(2.618 * x + 0.7)
        h0 = project_box_sum(h0, n0)
        best_c = compute_c5(h0, n0)
        best_h = h0

    selected = [(best_h, best_c)]
    selected_ns = set([int(len(best_h))])

    if time.time() < deadline - 70:
        base_h = best_h
        base_n = int(len(base_h))
        results = []
        uid = 0
        for n_try in candidate_ns_for(base_n):
            if time.time() > deadline - 40:
                break
            for mode in (0, 1):
                if n_try == base_n and mode != 0:
                    continue
                if mode == 0:
                    h_try = resample_nearest(base_h, base_n, n_try)
                else:
                    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, base_n)
                    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_try)
                    h_try = np.interp(x_new, x_old, base_h)
                h_try = project_box_sum(h_try, n_try)
                c_try = compute_c5(h_try, n_try)
                if c_try < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_h, best_c = h_try, c_try
                    base_h, base_n = best_h, int(len(best_h))
                results.append((c_try, int(n_try), uid, h_try))
                uid += 1

        if len(results) > 0:
            results.sort()
            for c_try, n_try, _uid, h_try in results:
                if int(n_try) in selected_ns:
                    continue
                if len(selected) >= 4:
                    break
                if c_try > best_c + 0.01 and len(selected) >= 2:
                    break
                selected.append((h_try, float(c_try)))
                selected_ns.add(int(n_try))

    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
    reserve = min(120.0, max(20.0, remaining * 0.15))
    avail = max(0.0, remaining - reserve)
    if len(selected) <= 1:
        budgets = [avail]
    else:
        fracs = (0.50, 0.30, 0.12, 0.08)
        budgets = []
        for i in range(len(selected)):
            budgets.append(avail * fracs[i] if i < len(fracs) else 0.0)
        caps = (420.0, 300.0, 220.0, 180.0)
        for i in range(len(budgets)):
            if i < len(caps):
                budgets[i] = min(budgets[i], caps[i])

    optimized_ns = set()
    for si in range(len(selected)):
        h_start, _cs = selected[si]
        n_start = int(len(h_start))
        if n_start in optimized_ns:
            continue
        if time.time() > deadline - 30:
            break
        if si < len(budgets):
            limit = time.time() + budgets[si]
        else:
            limit = deadline - reserve
        limit = min(limit, deadline - reserve)
        if limit <= time.time() + 20.0:
            continue
        h_opt, c_opt = optimize_at_n(h_start, n_start, limit)
        optimized_ns.add(n_start)
        if c_opt < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_h, best_c = h_opt, c_opt

    if time.time() < deadline - 150 and len(optimized_ns) < 4:
        base_n = int(len(best_h))
        best_res = None
        for n_try in candidate_ns_for(base_n):
            if n_try in optimized_ns:
                continue
            h_try = project_box_sum(resample_nearest(best_h, base_n, n_try), n_try)
            c_try = compute_c5(h_try, n_try)
            if c_try <= best_c + 0.002:
                if best_res is None or c_try < best_res[0]:
                    best_res = (float(c_try), int(n_try), h_try)
        if best_res is not None and time.time() < deadline - 100:
            _cr, nr, hr = best_res
            limit = min(deadline - reserve, time.time() + 300.0)
            h_opt, c_opt = optimize_at_n(hr, nr, limit)
            optimized_ns.add(nr)
            if c_opt < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_h, best_c = h_opt, c_opt

    if best_h is not None:
        n = int(len(best_h))
        if n <= 500 and time.time() < deadline - 15:
            c_arr = compute_all_lags(best_h, n)
            active = active_from_lags(c_arr, 5)
            h2, c2 = slsqp_active(best_h, n, active, 10000.0, 120)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_h, best_c = h2, c2

        if time.time() < deadline - 15:
            sub = min(deadline - 5.0, time.time() + 60.0)
            h2, c2 = projected_gradient_polish(best_h, n, sub, K=20, alphas=(500.0, 5000.0))
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_h, best_c = h2, c2

        if time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
            best_h, best_c = pair_local_search(best_h, n, deadline - 2.0)

        best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, int(len(best_h)))
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, len(best_h))

    if best_h is None or not np.isfinite(best_c):
        n = 300
        best_h = project_box_sum(np.full(n, 0.5), n)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(len(best_h))
```
